**Abstract.** The owner's six requirements (REQ-1..REQ-6) describe one tenant-admin journey: pick a
provider then a model, bind a governed prompt with typed context variables to an agent, compose
agents into a workflow, test before publishing, and publish for developers on both SDKs. Round 1
re-verified twelve load-bearing claims against `dev-2.2` and found six BLOCKERs. Round 2 applied the
owner's twelve answers and two further discovery lanes, which changed the design in five places:
a tenant's BYO connection now declares provider AND models together (materialised as tenant-owned
`AiModel` rows); the platform's engines and platform-keyed cloud rows are presented as ONE "Hope
provider" whose models carry live readiness; every inference activity is quota-checked and metered
(the production `POST /agents/:slug/invocations` route, the realtime `core.agent` lane and the
prompt test-run are unmetered today — a seventh, BLOCKER-class incidental); context schemas stay
tenant-owned and SYSTEM's schemas / agents / workflows / prompts are a REFERENCE SET cloned into a
tenant at creation (re-syncable, backfilled), never read across tenants; and deprecated code is
removed outright, not marked. Round 3 settled the remaining six: CONTENT is cloned and SYSTEM is
never a runtime fallback for it (the flip out of `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` is the LAST step of the
day-1 path, after every tenant is provisioned); the catalogue stays a SYSTEM reference with BYO rows
tenant-owned; platform settings are platform-admin-only; guardrail stays platform-managed with a
tenant opt-out per agent node / workflow; the register execution is TASK-901. The plan is fourteen
lanes in four waves with a fast-win critical path of ≈ 16 elapsed days (§4.8).

**Shipped (2026-09-07).** All fourteen lanes are merged. A tenant admin now picks a provider then
a model from a catalogue that shows BYO connections first and one "Hope provider" with live
readiness; binds a governed prompt with typed, declared variables through one picker shared by the
agents screen and the Studio; tests a DRAFT agent (metered) before publishing it; and publishes for
developers on both SDKs, with `@arcaai/vox` reduced to the business plane and `hope.admin.*` the
only administration surface. One template grammar renders identically in TypeScript and Python
against a shared fixture, and an unresolved variable fails closed by name. Guardrail stays
platform-managed with a per-node / per-workflow tenant opt-out that can only ever subtract, is
recorded on every call, and names every disabled mandatory node at publish. And the owner rule of
§1.5 is now true in code: **content is cloned, configuration cascades** — `Agent`,
`AgentAssignment`, `PromptTemplate` and `PromptVersion` left `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS`, every
tenant carries its own provisioned copy of the platform reference set, and a miss is a named
fail-closed error instead of a silent read of another tenant's rows.

---

## 1. Requirement Analysis

### 1.1 Product contract

| # | Owner requirement (verbatim intent) | Contract this plan delivers | "Met" means |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-1 | The tenant admin MUST be able to build and manage Agents (single inference task) and Workflows (combinations of tasks). | Agent and Workflow lifecycles stay as shipped (TASK-863/864); a publish is refused when what it freezes is not runnable; a DRAFT agent is testable and its model / instruction are editable before publish. | `POST /admin/workflow-definitions/:id/publish` refuses a `core.agent` node with no `agentRef`, an unparseable CEL, an unresolvable context-schema reference or an undeclared prompt variable; `POST /admin/agents/:id/test` answers a dry run for a DRAFT. |
| R-2 | The tenant admin MUST be able to publish Agents and Workflows for their developers / customers, integrating via `@arcaai/vox` (pure frontend) and `@arcaai/vox-node` (pure backend). | Both SDKs can discover a workflow's I/O contract, invoke published agents on an API key holding the business-plane scopes (OD-F), and decide a human review; `@arcaai/vox` carries NO management surface (its 22 `/admin/*` hook families are removed — OD-F + OD-K). | `hope.workflows.schema(slug)` and `hope.workflows.reviews.*` exist; `useAgentInvocation` exists; `GET /workflows` carries `inputSchema`/`outputSchema`/`protocols`/`triggerKinds`; `grep -r "/admin/" packages/agentic-sdk-v2/src` → none. |
| R-3 | When building or configuring an Agent node, it MUST allow the admin to select provider > models. | The picker lists the tenant's BYO connections first (each declares its models — OD-A), then ONE "Hope provider" (OD-L) whose models are the SYSTEM catalogue with live readiness. The agent row keeps `modelId` alone (TASK-863 D-2); BYO models are tenant-owned `AiModel` rows so the FK and the derived connection both hold. | `GET /admin/ai-models/catalogue?taskType=` answers a tenant admin with `providers[]` (BYO + `hope`) and `models[]` each carrying `readiness`; `PUT /admin/providers/:service/:provider/models` declares a BYO connection's models. |
| R-4 | Per inference task, each agent node MUST allow hyper-parameters, and instructions with context-variable injection via ONE templating syntax bound to the consultation-session context schema the admin defines per agent / workflow. | One grammar `{{ path.to.value \| default("…") }}` rendered identically by the gateway (TS) and the harness (Python), proven by a shared fixture; the context schema binds by reference on `Agent` and on `core.trigger` (inline stays — OD-G); schemas are TENANT-OWNED, seeded into a tenant from SYSTEM's reference set (OD-H); every reference is cross-validated at publish; an unresolved variable fails closed at render (OD-C). | The fixture passes on both sides; `Agent.contextSchemaId` exists and resolves TENANT-only; a `{{context.foo}}` that no bound schema declares is a publish finding; the realtime and durable lanes render one node the same way. |
| R-5 | The model registry / catalogue manages AND renders the available providers and models. ONLY the platform admin may manage it. | Registry writes stay `manage:all` + `assertPlatformAdmin()`; a read-only, picker-shaped catalogue is rendered to tenants under `read:AiModel` (OD-B); a tenant's BYO model rows are written ONLY through its connection screen, never through `/ai-models`; the per-tenant catalogue CLONE at tenant creation is retired (OD-O). | The 13 `/admin/ai-models*` routes are unchanged; the catalogue leaks no storage topology; `POST /admin/ai-models` still 403s a tenant admin; `provisionTenantModelCatalog` no longer exists. |
| R-6 | Each agent's instruction is selected from the prompt list with a quick view and a hyperlink to the prompt detail screen; the tenant admin manages prompts, mints versions, tests with a chosen provider + model + context variables; the management screen lists active / published prompts. | One shared `<PromptTemplatePicker>` (quick view + `/prompt-templates?template=<id>`), typed variable declarations, a variables form on the test panel, version pin + per-variable bindings when binding a prompt to an agent; the test-run is quota-checked and metered (OD-E). | Both pickers (agents, Studio) are the same component; the test panel sends `variables`; the agent stores `promptVersionNumber` + `variables` bindings from the UI; a test-run writes an `AiUsageEvent` with `trigger: PROMPT_TEST`. |

### 1.2 Invariants inherited (TASK-859 §3 inv-1..6, rule 00 §Configuration Principles, rule 09 §Tenant-first & BYO)

1. Configuration is never hardcoded and never an env var beyond the bootstrap floor; graphs and agents carry references, never engines, endpoints or keys. (The console's two hardcoded provider `Set`s in `create-agent-wizard.tsx:38-39` and the inventory cron's unregistered keys violate this today and are fixed by this ticket.)
2. Resolution is tenant → SYSTEM, two tiers, **for CONFIG**; `50000000-…` is a customer playground, never a fallback. (`provisionTenantConfigs` reads the Global tenant today — §2.7, OD-P.) **For CONTENT the cascade does not exist**: content is cloned into the tenant and SYSTEM is never read at runtime (§1.5, OD-M).
3. Fail closed on an unresolvable model, credential, availability — and, after this ticket, on an unresolvable prompt variable; degrade observably, never silently.
4. The gateway resolves; services execute. The harness gets a frozen snapshot in `compiledConfig`; it never re-reads a context schema from Postgres. Readiness probes are aggregated by `apps/text`; the gateway never opens an engine socket for inference.
5. A timeout never approves; the system never signs. (The review proxy forwards a HUMAN decision; it never decides.)
6. The browser captures and renders; it never runs a model. (Calling a server-side published agent from the browser on a scoped API key is not local inference — OD-F.)
7. Funding is derived, never stamped (`fundingOf`, `ai-provider-connection.service.ts:776-778`). Every metered path — production, test, workflow — derives it the same way (OD-E).
8. Entitlements bound, they never supply: plan-tier model gating (`model-access.ts:25-43`) filters what the catalogue SHOWS; it never materialises rows (OD-O).

### 1.3 Classification

`feature`, plus seven `bugfix` incidentals folded in because the feature is not trustworthy without them: the unwired publish gate (F-9a), the publish-availability read of the deprecated `localPath` / `downloadStatus` columns (F-9f), the realtime/durable render divergence (F-9j), the unmetered `POST /agents/:slug/invocations` + realtime lane + prompt test-run (§2.7 #6, OD-E), the tenant-creation clone that reads the Global customer tenant (§2.7 #5, OD-P), the SYSTEM-template fallback chain silently returned EMPTY by both the runtime resolver and the clone path (TASK-884 H-6, §2.7 #21 — fixed by L1 / L8, before the flip), and the legacy `GlobalSettingService` CRUD that has no SYSTEM-tier super-admin guard (§2.7 #24 — L1).

### 1.5 Owner rule established (2026-09-06): content is cloned, config cascades

| Class | Entities (exact) | Posture | Where SYSTEM is read |
|---|---|---|---|
| **CONTENT** — what a tenant authors, publishes and runs | `Agent` + `AgentModelFallback` + `AgentAssignment`; `PromptTemplate` + `PromptVersion`; `WorkflowDefinition` + `WorkflowAssignment`; `ConsultationContextSchema` + `ConsultationContextSchemaVersion` (and, by the same logic, `DocumentTemplate` + versions — already clone-only) | CLONED from the SYSTEM reference set at tenant creation (`TenantReferenceSetService`), thereafter TENANT-OWNED; re-sync is an explicit super-admin action; **SYSTEM is never read at runtime**. "Fallback" for an agent = its own `AgentModelFallback` chain (+ `parameters.fallback.agentSlug`), tenant-controlled. | ONLY by the provisioning / re-sync service and the super-admin library screens, through explicit unscoped reference reads (`findCloneSource` / `findSystemTemplates` pattern) — never through the tenant-scope extension's read widening |
| **CONFIG** — what the platform serves and the tenant may override | `AiProviderConnection`, `GlobalSetting` (platform settings), `TenantGuardrailPolicy`, `AiRoutingPolicy`, `AiModel` (the Hope catalogue), `AiPriceBook`, entitlements, `HarnessPolicy`, `McpServer`, `Role`, `TenantStorageConfig`, `TenantSttConfig`, `ServiceRelease` / `ServiceInstance` / `ChangelogEntry`, `WorkflowInvariantRule` | tenant → SYSTEM on ABSENCE (rule 00 §Configuration Principles), or SYSTEM-only where an owner decision says so (guardrail policy, routing policy, the catalogue registry, platform settings) | at runtime, through `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` or an explicit resolver that widens on absence |

Consequences: `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` ends up holding CONFIG models only (the four content models leave it — L13 step v); a runtime miss on content is a NAMED, fail-closed error (`AGENT_NOT_ASSIGNED`, `PROMPT_DEFAULT_NOT_PROVISIONED`, `LEGACY_CONTEXT_SCHEMA_MISSING`) that names the task and the tenant, never a silent SYSTEM read; provisioning is therefore a hard precondition of the flip, which is why the flip is the LAST step of the day-1 path (§4.8). Rule text lands with the ticket (§8 "rule amendments").

## 2. Current State Evaluation

### 2.1 Method

Four read-only discovery lanes (A: agent/model/provider backend; B: workflow node / execution / SDK; C: prompts / context schema / templating; D: console journeys), one evaluation pass that re-verified twelve claims against the code rather than against the reports, orchestrator spot-checks (`UpdateAgentRequest` accepts `modelId`/`instruction`/`tools`; both `admin/ai-models` controllers are class-gated `manage:all`; `GenerateRequest` declares only four generation fields; the realtime renderer is flat-key; every catalogue rule is `status: 'DRAFT'`), and — round 2 — two targeted lanes (E: BYO connections, `AiModel`, engine-served vs cloud, readiness probes, `AiRoutingPolicy`, seeds; F: tenant provisioning, `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS`, quota/metering per path, API-key scopes, the browser-SDK admin surface, the deprecation register, context-schema seeds) plus a six-point re-verification by this assessment (§2.7). Every path below was re-checked to exist on `dev-2.2` at 2026-09-06.

### 2.2 Gap matrix

Severity: **BLOCKER** = the journey cannot be completed; **MAJOR** = completes only with a workaround or unsafely; **MINOR** = quality.

### 2.3 The seven BLOCKERs

| Id | Defect | Lane that clears it |
|---|---|---|
| 1c | the workflow publish gate is unwired; DRAFT rules never block; no node-config schema check | L0 |
| 3c / 5c | no tenant can read the model catalogue (one defect, two requirements) | L1 (+ L5 for the picker) |
| 4d | six templating flavours; realtime vs durable divergence on the same node | L0 (grammar) + L3 (renderers) |
| 4e | no agent binds a context schema; publish cannot cross-validate `{{path}}` | L2 (columns, snapshot) + L8 (agent findings) + L0 (the check itself) + L13 (the tenant's schema exists) |
| 2e | a `core.humanReview` node has no gateway route | L7 |
| **§2.7 #6** (new) | `POST /agents/:slug/invocations`, the realtime `core.agent` lane and the prompt test-run are neither quota-checked nor metered — a production inference path that bills nothing (OD-E) | L11 (+ L4 for the prompt test, L8 for the draft test) |
| *(round 3 — on the day-1 path, not a journey blocker)* | OD-M's flip: the four content models leave `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` and the runtime SYSTEM tier for content is removed — the owner rule's definition of done, sequenced LAST because doing it early breaks every unprovisioned tenant silently (§2.7 #22, Risk 20) | L13 step v (after steps i–iv, the backfill and proof #9) |

### 2.4 The six templating flavours

| # | Grammar | File:line | Missing token | Runtime | Fate under this plan (OD-K) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `{var}` single brace, 9 fixed names | `packages/applications/src/services/consultation/prompt/pre-summary-variables.ts:105,124-128` | left as written | TS gateway | **DELETED** (`substitutePreSummaryVariables`); the seeds that carry `{var}` are converted to `{{context.var}}` |
| 2 | `{var}` single brace, hyphens allowed, second regex | `packages/applications/src/services/consultation/prompt/prompt-assembly.service.ts:23,104-108` | left as written | TS gateway (chain / comprehensive / pre-summary — six callers) | **DELETED** (`substituteVariables`); the hardcoded 5+9+11 name list (`:635-765`) is replaced by the tenant's `consultation-legacy-v1` schema clone (OD-H); the VALUE builders stay |
| 3 | `{{var}}` per declared key + a `{var}` fallback pass ("B2 brace-trap") | `packages/applications/src/services/prompt-management/prompt-management.service.ts:1164-1200` | left intact | TS gateway (prompt test) | replaced by `renderTemplate` (no dispatch, no sniff) |
| 4 | `{{var}}` flat key, dots accepted but NOT traversed | `packages/applications/src/services/agent/agent-invocation.service.ts:29-35` | left as written | TS gateway (`/agents/:slug/invocations`) | replaced by `renderTemplate` |
| 5 | `{{var}}` flat key — a fourth TS copy | `packages/applications/src/services/consultation/live-documentation/live-documentation.service.ts:176-182`, called at `:3384` | left as written | TS gateway (the REALTIME `core.agent` lane) | replaced immediately (correctness fix) |
| 6 | `{{path}}` dotted, `resolve_dotted_path` | `apps/harness/src/harness/temporal/interpreter/nodes/core.py:118,156-171`, applied at `:455` | left verbatim (documented) | Python harness (the DURABLE `core.agent` lane) | becomes the canonical engine, re-hosted in `templating.py` and held to the fixture |

Flavours 5 and 6 render the same node: durable builds `scope = {**context, **variables, "variables": variables}` and resolves dotted paths (`core.py:453-455`); realtime does `variables[key]` on `{ ...instruction.variables, ...overrides.promptVariables }` (`live-documentation.service.ts:3374,3384`), so `{{trigger.patientAge}}` resolves on one lane and is emitted as a literal on the other.

Seed census of the single-brace grammar (what L4 converts): `07-prompt-template.ts` 42 `{x}` / 13 `{{`; `07b-arcaai-clinical-content-v3.ts` 23 / 0; `07d-dept-free-pre-summary-default.ts` 21 / 0; `07a-agent-golden-library.ts` 4 / 0; `07c-live-agent-defaults.ts` 1 / 0.

### 2.5 What is right and must be kept

- The agent lifecycle and its fail-closed publish (`collectFindings`, `agent.service.ts:990-1046`; `hasBlocking`, `agent-findings.ts:37-39`) — this ticket ADDS findings to it, never a second validator.
- Capability gating (`hyperparameterCapabilityProblems`, `agentic-contract.ts:272-296`; the ERROR-vs-WARNING split "unknown ≠ unsupported").
- Reference-only bindings: `FORBIDDEN_CONFIG_KEYS` (`agentic-contract.ts:66-119`) and D-2 (no provider on the agent). A provider PICKER is a query parameter and a response field; it is not a stored key and does not collide with the ban.
- Prompt governance: `APPROVED` pins a `PromptVersion`; `resolvePrompt` (`agent.service.ts:1130-1208`) refuses an unapproved or missing version.
- The prompt test two-call machinery (`prompt-management.controller.ts:273-336`; `startPromptTemplateTest`, `resolveTestTextTarget` — `TextAgentResolverService`-backed, fail-closed, MISS-vs-ERROR split).
- The drawer deep link (`templates-tab.tsx:105`) and the `DetailDrawer` surface (`prompt-templates/components/agent-detail.tsx`).
- CEL and its parity fixture (`expressions.ts` / `expressions.py` / `__tests__/fixtures/expressions.fixture.json`, loaded by `expressions.test.ts:33` and `test_expressions_parity.py:23-37`) — the pattern this ticket copies for the template grammar.
- The Studio's `agentRef.slug` picker (`agent-picker-field.tsx`) and the `core.agent` overrides shape (`promptVariables`, `generation`, `carryForward` — `node-config-schemas.ts:1703-1765`).
- The three-state connection semantics (`CONNECTION_ENABLED_SEMANTICS`, `ai-provider-connection/constants.ts:148-155`) and `resolveConnection` (`ai-provider-connection.service.ts:296-311`) — the BYO design in §3.1/§3.7 adds a payload to the connection; it never adds a fourth state.
- TASK-884's clone machinery (`AgentService.clone/copyInto`, `agent.service.ts:464,861`; `WorkflowDefinitionService.clone/importDefinition/syncToTenants`, `workflow-definition.service.ts:410,575,814`; `portable-graph.ts`, `agent-bundle.ts`) — the reference-set service (§3.4) CALLS these; it does not re-implement copying.
- The speech handler's metering shape (`agent.controller.ts:245` precheck, `:290-306` `recordUsage` with `attributesJson` allow-listed) — the template every other path in §3.13 copies.

| Fact | Consequence |
|---|---|
| `packages/vox-node/src/core/webhook-signature.ts` already exports `verifyWebhookSignature` for OUTBOUND deliveries (`X-Hope-Webhook-Signature`, raw body). | The missing helper is a SIGNER for the inbound trigger (`"<timestamp>.<rawBody>"`, `X-Hope-Signature` + `X-Hope-Timestamp`), named `signWebhookTrigger` — P-11. |
| The harness mirrors `compiledConfig` with pydantic `extra="forbid"` (`compiled_config.py:198-245`) and the TS side has a normative JSON Schema with `additionalProperties: false` on `policyBindings` (`packages/workflow-contract/schemas/compiled-config.schema.json:266-268`, asserted by `compiled-config-schema.test.ts`). | A per-node context-schema stamp is an additive-optional field on BOTH sides with a default (the `documentTemplateRefs` precedent, `compiled_config.py:206-210`) — owned by one lane (L2). |
| `AiUsageEvent` carries `operation`, `provider`, `model`, `requestId` and `attributesJson` (`usage-ledger.prisma:51-113`); no `trigger` column; `attributesJson` is an ALLOW-LIST (`usageLedger/usage-attributes.ts:27-36`: `channelCount, engine, pipelineId, languageMode, serviceTier, interrupted, streamKind, cacheTtl, endpointKind, contextBand`). | The `trigger` dimension rides in `attributesJson` and MUST be added to the allow-list (`'string'`, enum-ish value regex `:61`) — L11; no schema change. |
| `service-account-scopes.registry.ts` / `apikey-scopes.registry.ts` exist; `@RequiredScopes` takes a raw string and the admin audit only enforces `FORBID` on `/admin/*` (`admin-scope-audit.ts` header). | `svc:admin:ai-model:read` must be added to the service-account registry (with `svc:admin:ai-model:manage` implying it) — L1 owns that file. |
| `apps/text/src/text/models/requests.py:172-180` — `provider: str = _HARDCODED_DEFAULT_PROVIDER` ("lm-studio") is a DOCUMENTED, TEST-PINNED known defect (`test_models_edge_cases.py:test_provider_defaults_to_lm_studio`), whose removal is blocked on two callers outside `apps/text` (`text-proxy.controller.ts:299`, harness `text_client.py:144`). | A rule-00 §1 violation already recorded as one; the agent path always sends `compiled.model.provider` (`agent-invocation.service.ts:90`; `core.py:602`). Follow-up TASK-895. |
| `agent.service.ts:99` `ENGINE_SERVED_PROVIDERS` is a module-local `Set` (exactly two occurrences repo-wide, `:99`, `:1113`); `ai-provider-connection/constants.ts:78,134` already hold `CLOUD_BYO_PROVIDERS` and `PLATFORM_SELF_HOST_PROVIDERS` (`llm` has NO self-host entry there). | The provider CLASS taxonomy the catalogue needs already has a home; L1 moves the engine set there and L8 repoints the publish gate. |
| `tenant-admin-authority.test.ts:95-217` pins every `action:subject` a route declares against what the seeded tenant-admin roles grant. | A new `read:AiModel` route fails that test until the seed rule lands — the two land together in L1 (Risk 8). |

## 3. Target Design

### 3.1 Data model deltas

| Change | Kind | Where |
|---|---|---|
| `Agent.contextSchemaId String?`, `Agent.contextSchemaVersionNumber Int?` | new COLUMNS (nullable, no FK across the version table — mirrors the `promptTemplateId` / `promptVersionNumber` pin); resolves TENANT-only (OD-H) | `packages/database/src/prisma/db_main/agent.prisma` |
| `AiModel.sourceConnectionId String?` + `@relation(fields: [sourceConnectionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)` + index | new COLUMN — the PROVENANCE of a tenant-materialised BYO model row (OD-A); a SYSTEM row never sets it; a connection cannot be hard-deleted while models point at it (the service soft-deletes them when the declaration shrinks or the connection is deleted) | `ai-model.prisma`, `ai-provider-connection.prisma` (back-relation `models AiModel[]`) |
| `AiModel.wireModelId` becomes the ROUTED id: NOT NULL for `deploymentKind IN (CLOUD, SELF_HOSTED-engine-served)`; data step `UPDATE core."AiModel" SET "wireModelId" = "sourceUri" WHERE "wireModelId" IS NULL AND provider IN ('lm-studio','lmstudio','ollama','vllm','llama-cpp')` | REPOINT (§2.7 #6) — `sourceUri` keeps its locator meaning (HF repo / `s3://`) for the inventory; `toTextCandidate` reads `wireModelId` | `ai-model.prisma` comment `:98-101`; `text-generation-spec.ts:122,129`; `packages/types/src/agent.ts` `ResolvedAgentModel.wireModelId` (new field) |
| `AiModel.downloadStatus`, `downloadedAt`, `fileSizeMb`, `localPath` + `enum AiModelDownloadStatus` | DROPPED (OD-K; the register said R3) — `localPath` survives ONLY as a derived wire field (`derivedLocalPath(bucketPrefix)`) on `ResolvedAgentModel` / `ResolvedAsrModel`, so `apps/stt` `spec.py:150,371` is untouched | `ai-model.prisma:141-153`, `enums.prisma`; readers per §2.7 #14 |
| `PromptTemplate.sourceTemplateId String?` (+ index), `PromptTemplate.templateLocked Boolean @default(false)`; `WorkflowDefinition.sourceTemplateSlug String?`, `WorkflowDefinition.templateLocked Boolean @default(false)` | new COLUMNS — the provenance pair `ConsultationContextSchema` / `DocumentTemplate` / `AsrPipeline` already carry (§2.7 #12), so the reference-set re-sync (§3.4) can tell a pristine clone from an edited one for EVERY cloned kind. **Round-3 correction:** `PromptTemplate` has NO slug (`prompt-template.prisma:98` `@@unique([tenantId, name])`), so its provenance key is the SYSTEM row's `id` — which is also what `SYSTEM_DEFAULTS.*` (`prompt-resolution.service.ts:333`) already hardcodes, so the post-flip resolver is `findFirst({ tenantId, sourceTemplateId: SYSTEM_DEFAULTS.promptId })` (§3.4) | `prompt-template.prisma`, `workflow-definition.prisma` |
| `PromptTemplate.variables` / `PromptVersion.variables` | stay `Json?`; the SHAPE becomes the validated array (§3.6); the seeds are converted to the array — NO read-side normaliser for the legacy map (OD-K) | DTO + service + seeds |
| `Agent.instruction` | stays `Json?`; `instruction.variables` is `{ [name]: { value } \| { path } }` — the flat string is NOT accepted (OD-K; the seed `25-agents.ts` is converted) | `agent-schemas.ts` |
| `Agent.compiledConfig` | stays `Json?`; gains `contextSchema: { schemaId, versionNumber, versionId, payloadSchema } \| null`, `model.wireModelId`, and the binding form of `instruction.variables` | `packages/types/src/agent.ts` `AgentCompiledConfig` |
| `WorkflowDefinition.compiledConfig` | stays `Json?`; `policyBindings.contextSchemaRefs[]` (additive-optional, default `[]`) + the compiled `core.trigger` node's `config.contextSchema.resolved` (the derived payload JSON Schema) | `compiler.ts`, `schemas/compiled-config.schema.json`, harness `compiled_config.py` |
| `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` | **(round 3, OD-M)** `Agent` (`:550`), `AgentAssignment` (`:551`), `PromptTemplate` (`:531`), `PromptVersion` (`:532`) are REMOVED — the LAST step of L13, wave 3, after the backfill and after every reader in §2.7 #22 is rewritten. `AgentModelFallback` is NOT added (round 2 retracted): H-6 is a CLS-context bug fixed by `runInTenantContext` in both readers (L1, L8), not a widening. `ConsultationContextSchema` / `…Version` stay EXCLUDED (OD-H). `AiModel` (`:384`) STAYS (catalogue = config, OD-O). | `packages/database/src/extensions/tenant-scope.ts:382-552` + `extensions/__tests__/tenant-scope.test.ts:401-528` (membership assertion `:478-490` + the `PromptTemplate read widening (B-12)` block `:499-528` are rewritten to assert the NEGATIVE) — L13 |
| Guardrail opt-out (§3.14) | **NO column.** The boolean rides in JSON that already exists: `Agent.parameters.guards.enabled` (`parameters Json`), `core.agent` / `core.trigger` node `config.guardrail.enabled` (`WorkflowDefinition.graph Json`), compiled into `Agent.compiledConfig.guardrail` and `WorkflowDefinition.compiledConfig.policyBindings.guardrail` (both `Json?`). The usage-row record is an `attributesJson` allow-list key (`guardrail`), no schema change. | — |
| Platform-settings gate (§3.15) | NO column; imperative guard only. | — |
| CASL | new ability `read:AiModel` granted to the tenant-admin policy that already holds `manage:Agent` (`seed/01-policy.ts:322`) and to `prompt-template-manage` (`:538-544`); the BYO model declaration reuses the ability the connection routes already require | `seed/01-policy.ts`, `seed/__tests__/tenant-admin-authority.test.ts` |
| NOT added (round 1 retracted) | `enum PromptSyntax` + `PromptVersion.syntax` — there is no second grammar to dispatch on once flavours 1-2 are deleted (OD-K) | — |
| NOT added | `AiProviderConnectionModel` child table — the tenant-owned `AiModel` row IS the declaration (one table, one fact); the connection DTO's `models[]` is a PROJECTION of the rows with `sourceConnectionId = row.id` | — |

Prisma sketch (exact section order per rule 02):

```prisma
// agent.prisma — after `modelId` (:95), before `instruction`
  /// The consultation-context schema this agent's prompt is written against (REFERENCE — the
  /// definition lives on the TENANT's row; the pinned version is frozen into `compiledConfig` at
  /// publish). Absent = the agent declares no context and `{{context.*}}` is a publish finding.
  contextSchemaId            String?
  contextSchemaVersionNumber Int?
  @@index([contextSchemaId], name: "Agent_contextSchemaId_idx")

// ai-model.prisma — after `deploymentKind` (:97)
  /// BYO provenance (TASK-890): the tenant's `AiProviderConnection` that DECLARED this model.
  /// NULL on every SYSTEM row. Set ⇒ tenant-owned, `deploymentKind = CLOUD`, `availability =
  /// NOT_APPLICABLE`, written ONLY by `AiProviderConnectionService.declareModels`, never by
  /// `/admin/ai-models`. `Restrict`: the connection cannot vanish under its models.
  sourceConnectionId String?
  sourceConnection   AiProviderConnection? @relation(fields: [sourceConnectionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  @@index([sourceConnectionId], name: "AiModel_sourceConnectionId_idx")

// prompt-template.prisma (PromptTemplate) and workflow-definition.prisma — after the core fields
  /// Golden-library provenance (the `ConsultationContextSchema` pair): the SYSTEM row this one
  /// was cloned from; `templateLocked` = a re-sync may overwrite it (pristine clone).
  sourceTemplateSlug String?
  templateLocked     Boolean @default(false)
```

One migration, authored by L2 on a throwaway shadow DB (rule 02 recipe, §4.1 L2), named `task_890_context_schema_byo_model_provenance`. Its data steps: the `wireModelId` backfill above; `UPDATE core."AiModel" SET "localPath" = NULL` is unnecessary (the column drops); NO prompt-syntax classification (retracted). Pre-production: the seeds are the population, so single-brace `PromptVersion` rows are replaced by `pnpm db:seed` re-upserting the converted seeds, and the pre-merge proof #4 (§4.4) asserts zero rows still match `\{[A-Za-z_][\w-]*\}` without `\{\{`.

### 3.2 The template grammar (one grammar, two renderers, one fixture)

```
template    := ( text | escape | placeholder )*
escape      := "{{{{"                                   ; renders the literal "{{"
placeholder := "{{" WS? path ( WS? "|" WS? filter )? WS? "}}"
path        := ident ( "." ident )*
ident       := [A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*
filter      := "default" WS? "(" string ")"             ; the ONLY filter
string      := '"' ( [^"\\] | "\\" . )* '"'
WS          := [ \t]+
```

| Rule | Semantics |
|---|---|
| Resolution | `path` is resolved against the scope object by dotted traversal (plain objects only; arrays are values, not traversable by index). |
| Missing | a path that does not resolve (or resolves to `null`/`undefined`) with no `default` → **named error** `PromptVariableUnresolved { path, templateRef }`; with `default("…")` → the default string. |
| Values are data | exactly one pass; a `{{` inside a substituted value is never re-interpreted (promotes `pre-summary-variables.ts:110-118` to the contract before that file is deleted). |
| Non-string values | JSON-serialised with sorted keys (`json.dumps(..., sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)` ≡ canonical `JSON.stringify` with sorted keys) — byte-identical across languages. |
| Whitespace | `{{ a.b }}`, `{{a.b}}`, `{{ a.b | default("x") }}` are the same placeholder. |
| Prototype keys | `constructor`, `__proto__`, `toString` etc. are not variables (own-property lookup), same as `pre-summary-variables.ts:117`. |
| Malformed | an unterminated `{{`, an invalid ident, an unknown filter → `templateSyntaxProblems()` at publish (ERROR); at render the renderer throws `PromptTemplateSyntaxError`. |

Ownership:

| Piece | Path | Exports |
|---|---|---|
| TS renderer + checker | `packages/workflow-contract/src/template.ts` (new) | `renderTemplate(content, scope, opts?)`, `templateReferences(content): TemplateReference[]` (`{ path, hasDefault, offset }`), `templateSyntaxProblems(content): string[]`, `templateReferenceProblems(content, declared: DeclaredNamespaces): string[]`, `PromptVariableUnresolvedError`, `PromptTemplateSyntaxError` |
| Python mirror | `apps/harness/src/harness/temporal/interpreter/templating.py` (new) | `render_template`, `template_references`, `template_syntax_problems`, `PromptVariableUnresolved`, `PromptTemplateSyntaxError` |
| Conformance fixture | `tests/contracts/prompt-template.fixture.json` (new) | `{ "cases": [{ name, template, scope, expected \| error }] }` |
| Loaders | `tests/contracts/prompt-template-parity.contract.test.ts` (new) — the `resolved-asr-spec-parity.contract.test.ts:22-27` loader shape; `apps/harness/src/harness/tests/unit/temporal/interpreter/test_templating_parity.py` (new) — the `test_expressions_parity.py:23-37` `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[8] / "tests" / "contracts" / …` shape | one Vitest, one pytest |
| Syntax dispatch | **none** (round 1's `prompt-renderer.ts` is retracted — OD-K): every TS caller imports `renderTemplate` from `@arcaai/workflow-contract` directly | — |

Fixture case list (the minimum the fixture must carry; both loaders assert it is not vacuous):

1. `dotted-resolution` — `{{trigger.patient.age}}` over a nested scope.
2. `bare-name` — `{{department}}` from a declared variable.
3. `missing-path-errors` — `{{context.absent}}` → error `PromptVariableUnresolved`.
4. `default-applied` — `{{context.absent | default("n/a")}}` → `n/a`.
5. `default-not-applied-when-present` — value wins over default.
6. `escape` — `{{{{` renders `{{`.
7. `value-with-braces-not-reparsed` — a value containing `{{x}}` is emitted verbatim.
8. `whitespace-tolerance` — `{{ a.b }}` ≡ `{{a.b}}`.
9. `object-value-json-sorted` — `{{vars.obj}}` → `{"a":1,"b":[1,2]}` (sorted keys, no spaces).
10. `array-not-indexable` — `{{vars.list.0}}` → error.
11. `null-is-missing` — a `null` leaf → error (default applies).
12. `unknown-filter-is-syntax-error` — `{{a | upper}}` → syntax error.
13. `unterminated-is-syntax-error` — `{{a` → syntax error.
14. `prototype-key-not-variable` — `{{constructor}}` → error, not a function.
15. `nodes-namespace` — `{{nodes.n1.text}}`.
16. `single-brace-is-literal` — `{language_name}` renders VERBATIM (no fallback pass; the deleted grammar is not silently honoured).

### 3.3 The runtime variable namespace (one vocabulary, both call shapes, both languages)

| Root | Workflow run (`core.agent`, durable AND realtime) | Standalone invocation (`POST /agents/:slug/invocations`), draft test | Declared by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trigger.*` | the `core.trigger`-validated run payload | — | the trigger's context schema (reference or inline) |
| `context.*` | alias of `trigger.*` when a context schema is bound (so one prompt is portable between the two call shapes) | the request's `context` object, validated against the agent's bound context schema | the agent's / trigger's context schema |
| `input.*` | — | the body validated against `inputSchema` | `inputSchema` |
| `vars.*` | merged `core.variable` outputs | — | graph (`core.variable` nodes) |
| `nodes.<id>.*` | prior node outputs | — | graph |
| bare `name` | the agent's `instruction.variables` bindings, overlaid by `overrides.promptVariables` (node wins) | same, overlaid by the request's `variables` (caller wins) | `instruction.variables` (+ the template's declared variables) |

A binding `{ path: "context.patientAge" }` is resolved from the scope BEFORE the bare-name overlay, so `{{age}}` and `{{context.patientAge}}` are the same value. This is the CEL root set (`EXPRESSION_CONTEXT_ROOTS`, `expressions.ts:719`: `trigger`, `vars`, `nodes`) plus `context` and `input`.

Publish-time cross-validation (`templateReferenceProblems(content, declared)`): every reference must resolve to a declared namespace root with a declared field, a declared bare variable, or carry `default(...)`. `declared` is built by the caller: for an agent — the bound context schema's derived payload schema (`context.*`, `trigger.*`), `inputSchema` (`input.*`), `instruction.variables` names + the template's declared variables (bare); for a graph — the trigger's schema, `core.variable` keys (`vars.*`), upstream node output ports (`nodes.<id>.*`), and per `core.agent` node the referenced agent's declared names plus `overrides.promptVariables` keys. Unknown-shaped roots (an inline schema with `additionalProperties: true`) admit any sub-path under that root.

### 3.4 Context-schema binding: reference (governed default) + inline (ad hoc) — tenant-owned, seeded from SYSTEM's reference set (OD-G, OD-H, OD-J)

| Consumer | Reference | Inline | Publish resolution | Runtime |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Agent` | `contextSchemaId` + `contextSchemaVersionNumber` (new columns) | `inputSchema` stays as is | L8: the row must be the CALLER TENANT's (`findById` under tenant CLS; a SYSTEM or foreign id → 404 → finding `CONTEXT_SCHEMA_NOT_FOUND`), the version must exist (`CONTEXT_SCHEMA_VERSION_NOT_FOUND`); the derived payload schema is frozen into `compiledConfig.contextSchema.payloadSchema` | `AgentInvocationService` validates `body.context` against the frozen schema (TIER 3, `jsonSchemaValueProblems`) and exposes `context.*` |
| `core.trigger` | `contextSchema.contextSchemaId` + `versionNumber` (already authorable) | `contextSchema.inline` (already validated) | L2: same TENANT-only resolution; the compiler writes `config.contextSchema.resolved` (the derived payload schema) on the compiled trigger node and appends `{ nodeId, schemaId, versionNumber, versionId }` to `policyBindings.contextSchemaRefs` | harness `interpreter_core_trigger` validates against `resolved ?? inline` (3-line change in `nodes/core.py:203-214`, landed by L3 which owns the file) — no DB read, invariant 4 |

Derived payload schema: an object schema keyed by kind `key`, each `STRUCTURED` kind contributing its `fields` (the same `kind.fields` `validateContextPayload` applies per kind, `consultation-context-schema.service.ts:382-386`), non-STRUCTURED kinds contributing `{ type: 'object' }` reference stubs. Implemented once as `payloadSchemaFromDefinition(definition)` in `context-schema-definition.ts` (L2) and used by publish (agent + workflow) and by the console's variable chips.

**No shared read, no runtime SYSTEM tier for schemas.** `ConsultationContextSchema` stays out of `SYSTEM_SHARED_READ_MODELS` (`tenant-scope.ts:263-269` already records why). A SYSTEM agent or workflow that references a SYSTEM schema is CLONED into a tenant with the reference REWRITTEN to the tenant's own clone (matched by `sourceTemplateSlug`) — the `agent-bundle.ts` re-resolve-by-name discipline.

**The reference set (`TenantReferenceSetService`, `packages/applications/src/services/tenant/reference-set/` (new)).** Named after the house `provisionTenant*` / `pipeline-template-resync` pattern; it is a CALLER of the existing clone machinery, never a second copier.

| Kind | Source (SYSTEM rows) | Copy routine | Provenance stamped | Assignment written |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Context schemas + versions | every ENABLED, published SYSTEM `ConsultationContextSchema` (first: `consultation-legacy-v1`, seed `07g`) | new `ConsultationContextSchemaService.cloneFromSystem(slug, tenantId)` over `create()` (`:88`, already accepts `sourceTemplateSlug`/`templateLocked`) + `publish()` (`:205`) + `pin()` (`:285`) | `sourceTemplateSlug`, `templateLocked: true` | `isDefault` where the SYSTEM row is default |
| Prompt templates + versions (approved pin) | the golden library (`07a`, 13) + `07c` live defaults + the `SYSTEM_DEFAULTS.*` rows (`07`, `07d`) | new `PromptManagementService.cloneFromSystem(templateId, tenantId)` — `createPromptTemplate` (`:236`) + version mint + `approveTemplate` (`:484`) at the SYSTEM row's approved version; the SYSTEM row is read through `PromptTemplateRepository.findSystemReferenceById(id, client)` (new, unscoped — §2.7 #23) | `sourceTemplateId` = the SYSTEM row's id (new; §3.1), `templateLocked: true` | — |

The tenant's control is the per-node / per-workflow OPT-OUT (§3.14), not a policy row | — | — |

Each names the re-sync route as the remedy. Proof #9 (zero unprovisioned tenants per kind) gates the flip | fail closed, named — never a SYSTEM read |

**Legacy bridge (OD-H, revised).** `consultation-legacy-v1` is a SYSTEM-owned `ConsultationContextSchema` seeded by `seed/07g-consultation-legacy-context-schema.ts` (new; exemplar `07f-arcaai-department-context-schemas.ts`) whose single `STRUCTURED` kind `context` declares exactly the names `PromptAssemblyService.buildVariables` populates (`conversation_language`, `ner_entities`, `clinician_notes`, `attachments`, `doctor_highlights`, the nine v1 names, `pre_summary_text`, `prior_visit_summary`, the `style_DNA_*` keys). It is CLONED into every tenant by the reference set; `PromptAssemblyService` reads the TENANT's clone (`findFirst({ tenantId, sourceTemplateSlug: 'consultation-legacy-v1' })`) and populates `context.*` from the same value builders it already runs. The code-owned NAME list (`:635-765`) is deleted by L13; the VALUE builders stay.

**Retirements folded into the same lane (L13 — decided by OD-O / OD-P, round 3):** `provisionTenantModelCatalog` (`tenant.service.ts:83,232-307`) is deleted — the catalogue is read-only and SYSTEM-only (OD-B, OD-O, TASK-860), plan-tier gating moves into the catalogue filter (`modelAllowedForTier`, `model-access.ts:33-43`, applied to the SYSTEM rows the route returns — entitlements bound, never supply); a backfill soft-deletes non-SYSTEM `AiModel` rows with `sourceConnectionId IS NULL` (clones) after proof #3. `provisionTenantConfigs` (`:62,576-641`) is deleted — the runtime cascade (tenant → SYSTEM, `AppSettingsService`) answers on absence — after proof #7 (§4.4) shows every `GlobalSetting` reader widens; if proof #7 finds a pinned reader, the fallback is a one-line repoint to `SYSTEM_TENANT_ID` and the reader is filed as a bug. `provisionTenantPipelineCatalog` is NOT touched here (OD-N, TASK-901).

**No runtime SYSTEM fallback for content (OD-M, round 3) — the ordered mechanics.** The owner rule (§1.5) makes SYSTEM a reference set, not a tier. Discovery H (§2.7 #22) showed the flip has two failure modes if done early — an explicit-`tenantId` read CRASHES (`AgentAssignmentRepository.findAllForScope` under `mergeTenantIntoWhere`), an implicit one goes SILENT (`AgentRepository`'s visible family, `PromptTemplateRepository.findById`) — so L13 runs these steps in this order, each with its own gate, and step v is the last commit of the day-1 path:

### 3.5 The publish-findings contract (F-10 — one name, one enforcement point)

```ts
// packages/workflow-contract/src/publish-findings.ts (new)
export interface PublishContext {
  registry?: WorkflowNodeRegistry;
  /** Per referenced agent slug: its declared variable names + bound context payload schema, resolved by the caller (the service). */
  agents?: Record<string, { declaredVariables: string[]; contextPayloadSchema: JsonSchema | null; generationRanges?: GenerationRanges }>;
  /** The resolved trigger context payload schema when `contextSchemaId` is used; null when unresolved (a finding is emitted). */
  triggerContextSchema?: JsonSchema | null;
  /** First release: 'WARNING'; second release: 'ERROR' (P-4, OD-C). */
  templateReferenceSeverity: 'WARNING' | 'ERROR';
}
export function publishFindings(graph: WorkflowGraph, ctx: PublishContext): WorkflowFinding[];
```

`WorkflowFinding` (`report.ts:13-24`) gains an optional `code?: string`. Codes (shared vocabulary with `AgentFindingCode`, `agent-findings.ts:6-16`, which gains the last three):

| Code | Source | Severity |
|---|---|---|
| `NODE_CONFIG_SCHEMA` | `jsonSchemaValueProblems(descriptor.configSchema, node.config)` per `core.*` node (the check nobody runs today) | ERROR |
| `AGENT_REF_MISSING`, `CEL_INVALID`, `BRANCH_HANDLE`, `LOOP_BOUNDS`, `OUTPUT_PROTOCOL`, `ACTION_KEY`, `LOOP_BODY`, `PORT_BINDING`, `NODE_CONTRACT`, `GUARD_REQUIRED` | the existing `workflowPublishProblems` parts (`port-validation.ts:245-300`), re-emitted as findings instead of strings | ERROR |
| `OVERRIDE_OUT_OF_RANGE` | `core.agent.overrides.generation` vs the agent's declared ranges (4c) | ERROR |
| `CONTEXT_SCHEMA_NOT_FOUND`, `CONTEXT_SCHEMA_VERSION_NOT_FOUND` | the trigger's / agent's reference did not resolve IN THE TENANT | ERROR |
| `PROMPT_VARIABLE_UNDECLARED` | `templateReferenceProblems` | `ctx.templateReferenceSeverity` (ramp, P-4) |
| `PROMPT_TEMPLATE_SYNTAX` | `templateSyntaxProblems` | ERROR |

Wiring: `WorkflowDefinitionService.publishEntity` calls `publishFindings` after the shape check and before compile (`workflow-definition.service.ts:1224-1247`), records every finding on `validationReport.findings` (so the Studio rail renders them) and refuses on `hasBlockingFindings`; `validate()` (`:1173`) records them without refusing. `AgentService.collectFindings` (`agent.service.ts:990`) appends `templateSyntaxProblems` + `templateReferenceProblems` over the resolved prompt and the context-schema findings. First release: enforcement scoped to `core.*` nodes (Risk 4). `workflowPublishProblems(): string[]` is DELETED (OD-K) and its five callers (`packages/database/scripts/regen-example-consultation-workflow-seed.ts`, `seed/23-arcaai-workflow-authoring.ts`, four seed tests) are repointed to `publishFindings` by L0.

### 3.6 Typed prompt variables and the picker projection

```ts
// packages/applications/src/services/prompt-management/dto/prompt-variable-declaration.dto.ts (new)
export class PromptVariableDeclarationDto {
  @IsString() @Matches(/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/) name: string;
  @IsIn(['string','number','boolean','date','json']) type: 'string'|'number'|'boolean'|'date'|'json';
  @IsBoolean() required: boolean;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(4000) default?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(500) description?: string;
  @IsOptional() @ValidateNested() source?: { kind: 'context'|'static'; path?: string };
}
```

`CreatePromptTemplateRequest.variables` / `UpdatePromptTemplateRequest.variables` become `PromptVariableDeclarationDto[]` (`@ValidateNested({ each: true })`); the seeds' legacy map (`{ name: { type, required } }`, `seed/07-prompt-template.ts:437-500`) is CONVERTED to the array by L4 and `extractDeclaredVariableNames` (`prompt-management.service.ts:137-149`) drops its map branch (OD-K). The list response (`GET /admin/prompt-templates`) gains `contentPreview` (server-truncated, 400 chars) and `declaredVariables: PromptVariableDeclarationDto[]`; the full `content` stays on `GET :id`. `TestPromptTemplateRequest.variables` is validated against the declarations (required present, types coerced) and rendered through `renderTemplate`.

### 3.7 The tenant catalogue: BYO connections + the Hope provider, with live readiness (OD-A, OD-B, OD-L)

```
GET /api/v1/admin/ai-models/catalogue?taskType=&providerGroup=&usableOnly=
@ApiTags('admin-ai-models')  @ForbidApiKey()  @RequiredSvcScopes('svc:admin:ai-model:read')  @Authorize(['read', 'AiModel'])
```

Registered in `apps/api/src/modules/ai-model/ai-model.module.ts` BEFORE `AiModelAdminController` (the module comment: static paths must register before the `:id` family). Reuses the existing `admin-ai-models` tag (`apps/api/src/openapi/tags.ts:204-208`); no new tag.

```ts
// packages/applications/src/services/ai-model/dto/model-catalogue.response.ts (new)
export type ProviderGroup = 'byo' | 'hope';
export type ProviderClass = 'cloud-byo' | 'cloud-platform' | 'engine-served' | 'platform-self-host';
export type ModelReadiness = 'ready' | 'loadable' | 'engine_down' | 'weights_missing' | 'credential_missing' | 'unknown';
export class CatalogueProviderResponse {
  /** `byo:<service>:<provider>` (one per enabled BYO connection) or `hope` (exactly one). */
  id: string; group: ProviderGroup; name: string; providerClass: ProviderClass | null /* null for the hope group */;
  connectionId: string | null; usable: boolean; reason: string | null; modelCount: number;
}
export class CatalogueModelResponse {
  id: string; slug: string; name: string; description: string | null; taskType: ModelTaskType; pipelineTag: string;
  providerId: string /* the CatalogueProviderResponse.id this model sits under */; provider: string /* the engine/vendor id, kept for display */;
  providerClass: ProviderClass; deploymentKind: AiDeploymentKind; availability: AiModelAvailability;
  readiness: ModelReadiness; readinessCheckedAt: Date | null; readinessDetail: string | null;
  capabilities: { supportedGenerationParams?: string[]; supportsSsml?: boolean }; isPlatformDefaultFor: AiTaskKind[];
  resourceStatus: ResourceStatusType; usable: boolean; unusableReason: string | null;
}
export class ModelCatalogueResponse { providers: CatalogueProviderResponse[]; models: CatalogueModelResponse[]; unassignedProviderCount?: number /* super admin only */; }
```

Nothing from `ModelResponse` (`model.response.ts`) that names storage or operator identity crosses over: `sourceUri`, `sourceRevision`, `wireModelId`, `bucketPrefix`, `primaryObject`, `manifestDigest`, `availabilityDetail`, `checksum`, `memorySizeMb`, `computeType`, `createdBy`, `updatedBy`, `tenantId`, `sourceConnectionId` are excluded by construction (the mapper builds the DTO field by field; the unit test asserts the key set).

**Two groups, in picker order (OD-A):**

| Group | Rows | Usability | Readiness |
|---|---|---|---|
| `byo` — one provider entry per ENABLED tenant `AiProviderConnection` for the task's service | the tenant's own `AiModel` rows with `sourceConnectionId = connection.id` (declared through §3.7a) — the tenant sees its BYO models ONLY under its BYO provider; a BYO slug shadows a SYSTEM slug (`resolveModelIdBySlugVisible`, `agent.service.ts:758-765`) | `usable` = the connection resolves with a key (`resolveConnection` non-null AND `encryptedApiKey` present — the "resolves but never delivers" split, discovery E §cross-cutting 3) | `ready` when the last connection probe (§3.12) succeeded and the model appeared in the vendor list (`discoveredModels`), `credential_missing` when keyless, `unknown` when never probed — a BYO model is never `engine_down` |
| `hope` — exactly ONE entry, "Hope provider" (OD-L) | every SYSTEM `AiModel` row for the task (plan-tier filtered by `modelAllowedForTier(tags, modelTierForPlan(tenant.plan))` — entitlements bound, never supply), grouped for DISPLAY only; `AiModel.provider` keeps the engine / vendor id for routing and the ledger (`KNOWN_PROVIDERS`, `AI_MODEL_PROVIDERS` and the parity test `tests/contracts/ai-model-providers.contract.test.ts:28-30` are UNCHANGED — `hope` is a DTO label, never a provider id) | per `providerClass`, below | per `providerClass`, from the §3.12 snapshot; `readinessCheckedAt` carries the snapshot time ("at that point of time") |

`providerClassOf(service, provider, row)` (`ai-provider-connection/constants.ts`, L1 — `ENGINE_SERVED_PROVIDERS` moves here from `agent.service.ts:99`):

| Class | Membership | `usable` test | Readiness derivation (§3.12) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cloud-byo` | tenant row with `sourceConnectionId` | the owning connection is enabled + keyed | connection probe |
| `cloud-platform` | SYSTEM row, `deploymentKind = CLOUD`, provider ∈ `CLOUD_BYO_PROVIDERS[service]` (e.g. `azure-gpt-5.4-mini`) | `resolveConnection(service, provider, tenantId)` non-null AND keyed (tenant row wins, SYSTEM row on absence, `null` on veto — `ai-provider-connection.service.ts:296-311`) AND the tenant's entitlement `featurePlatformDefaultCredential` admits the SYSTEM credential; reason `no-enabled-connection` / `platform-credential-not-entitled` | `ready` when the SYSTEM connection's last probe succeeded, else `credential_missing` / `unknown` |
| `engine-served` | SYSTEM row, provider ∈ `ENGINE_SERVED_PROVIDERS` (`lm-studio`, `lmstudio`, `ollama`, `vllm`, `llama-cpp`) | the SYSTEM engine connection is enabled | `ready` (engine up, model `state: loaded` — LM Studio `/api/v0/models`, Ollama `/api/ps`), `loadable` (engine up, model listed, not resident; vLLM / llama.cpp: listed ⇒ ready), `weights_missing` (engine up, model absent from the listing — discovery's `registered-missing-on-server`), `engine_down` (probe timeout / error), `unknown` (cold cache) |
| `platform-self-host` | SYSTEM row, provider = `built-in` OR provider ∈ `PLATFORM_SELF_HOST_PROVIDERS[service]` (`kokoro`, `indic_parler`, `indic_f5`) — served by `apps/stt` / `apps/nlp` / `apps/tts` from the bucket | `availability === 'AVAILABLE'` (the measured column, never `localPath`) | `ready` when `availability = AVAILABLE` AND the `servedBy` service heartbeat is healthy (`monitoring:heartbeat:<servedBy>`), `weights_missing` when `MISSING`/`PARTIAL`, `engine_down` when the service heartbeat is stale, `unknown` when `availability = UNKNOWN` (inventory never ran) |

Rows with `provider IS NULL` or a value outside `AI_MODEL_PROVIDERS` (`ai-model/constants.ts:17-35`) are hidden from tenants and counted in `unassignedProviderCount` for a super admin (Risk 6). The route replaces the prompt test panel's dependency on the playground `/text/providers` (`shared/catalog/hooks.ts:76-83`). Publish (L8) applies the SAME class table: a `usable === false` model is `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`; readiness is advisory at publish (an engine that is down now may be up at run time) and fail-closed at RUN time by the existing 503 (`connection.py:55-70`).

**3.7a BYO declaration — provider + model together (OD-A).**

```
PUT /api/v1/admin/providers/:service/:provider/models      (same class gate as the connection routes; tenant row only — a SYSTEM connection 403s "declare platform models in /admin/ai-models")
  body DeclareConnectionModelsRequest { models: [{ wireModelId: string; name: string; taskType: ModelTaskType /* ∈ the service's task types */; capabilities?: { supportedGenerationParams?: string[]; supportsSsml?: boolean } }] }
  200 AiProviderConnectionResponse (+ `models[]` projection)
GET  /api/v1/admin/providers/:service/:provider            → gains `models[]` (projection of the tenant rows with `sourceConnectionId`)
POST /api/v1/admin/providers/:service/:provider/test       → `TestProviderConnectionResponse` gains `discoveredModels: string[]` (the vendor list the probe already fetches and discards — `provider-connection-probe.ts:212-233`, `:141`, `:194-197`) so the console offers "derive models from provider"
```

`AiProviderConnectionService.declareModels(service, provider, dto)` (L10) materialises one tenant-owned `AiModel` per entry — `tenantId = tenant`, `slug = <provider>-<slugify(wireModelId)>` (server-generated, stable; **round 3, P-29: a generated slug that already exists under SYSTEM is REFUSED with 409 `BYO_SLUG_SHADOWS_PLATFORM { slug, systemModelId }` and the suggested `byo-<provider>-<slugify(wireModelId)>` — the declaration never silently replaces a Hope row for every by-slug resolver in that tenant, §2.7 #25**), `provider = connection.provider`, `wireModelId`, `sourceUri = wireModelId` (locator = wire id for a vendor model), `deploymentKind = CLOUD`, `availability = NOT_APPLICABLE`, `servedBy = 'text' | 'stt' | 'tts'` by service, `libraryName` per the vendor, `sourceConnectionId = row.id` — through `AiModelRepository` directly (the `AiModelService` admin surface stays SYSTEM-pinned, REQ-5); entries removed from the list are `softDelete`d (an agent bound to one keeps its FK — `onDelete: Restrict` — and its next publish fails `MODEL_DISABLED`, observably). Disabling the connection (the VETO state) leaves the rows and makes them `usable: false` (`no-enabled-connection`); deleting the connection soft-deletes its rows first. D-2 holds: the agent stores `modelId`; the connection is derived at resolve time from `(task → service, model.provider)` exactly as today, and a tenant row's provider IS its connection's provider by construction. Funding: `fundingOf(row)` on the resolved CONNECTION (`:776-778`) — a tenant row resolves the tenant connection → `tenant`; nothing stamps.

Wire consequence (§2.7 #6): `toTextCandidate` (`text-generation-spec.ts:116-137`) sends `model.wireModelId`; a candidate whose model carries `sourceConnectionId` OMITS `deployment_name` from its override entry (the row IS the deployment), so `azure_openai.py:164-175` forwards `request.model`; the SYSTEM Azure row keeps today's behaviour (the SYSTEM connection's `deploymentName` names the platform deployment). One contract test in `apps/text` (`test_azure_deployment_precedence_task890.py`, new) pins both branches.

The five console cards that already name ONE model (`provider-meta.ts:66-127`: `extraJson.model`, `deploymentName`) keep working — `declareModels` with one entry is the same fact; L10 migrates those cards to the models editor and the STT/embeddings `extraJson.model` readers (`asr-agent-resolver.service.ts:127-146`; `ai-provider-connection.service.ts:652-655`) keep reading the extra, which the editor writes for a single-model service.

### 3.8 The draft-agent test route (F-7) — metered (OD-E)

```
POST /api/v1/admin/agents/:id/test            @CanManage('Agent')  @ForbidApiKey()  @Throttle({ heavy: { limit: 20, ttl: 60000 } })
  body TestAgentRequest { input?: Record<string, unknown>; variables?: Record<string, unknown>; context?: Record<string, unknown>;
                          provider?: string; model?: string /* paired, verbatim, validated like the prompt test */; dryRun?: boolean /* default true */ }
  200 AgentTestAckResponse
    | { mode: 'dry-run', findings: AgentFinding[], assembledSystemPrompt: string | null, assembledUserPrompt: string,
        resolved: { provider: string; model: string; fundingTier: 'platform' | 'tenant'; source: 'row' | 'override' } }
    | { mode: 'stream', …same…, taskId: string, streamUrl: string }
POST /api/v1/admin/agents/:id/test/finalize   @CanManage('Agent')  @ForbidApiKey()
  body { taskId }  → { output: string; usage: …; provider; model }   (read back SERVER-SIDE by taskId, never from the client)
```

- Compiles the DRAFT in memory through the same `collectFindings` + `compile` path publish uses (`agent.service.ts:1218-1250`), never persisting `compiledConfig`; blocking findings answer 400 with the findings (the same shape the console already renders).
- Renders the prompt with §3.2 over the §3.3 scope; an unresolved variable is a 400 naming the path — the cheapest possible answer to "why is my prompt wrong". A dry run generates NOTHING and meters nothing.
- Non-dry runs (TEXT_GENERATION only in this ticket) call `assertMeterQuota(tenantId, 'monthlyLlmTokens')` (no-increment shape, `summary.service.ts:376-382`) BEFORE submitting, submit the streaming job exactly as `startPromptTemplateTest` does (`prompt-management.service.ts:913-947`) with `externalWrite` suppressed, and on finalize record `generate.stream` through `buildLlmUsageInput` (`text-usage.ts:143`) with `attributesJson.trigger = 'AGENT_TEST'` (§3.13). ASR / TTS drafts answer dry-run only (the resolved spec preview).
- Funding is derived as production does (`text-agent-resolver.service.ts:265-298`). Never a stamped `funding: 'test'`. (P-10 reversed: the test COUNTS against the tenant's quota — OD-E.)
- A PUBLISHED row is refused (409: "use `POST /agents/:slug/invocations`"); a foreign or SYSTEM row is 404.

| Need | Gateway / applications | `@arcaai/vox-node` | `@arcaai/vox` |
|---|---|---|---|
| Discover a workflow's I/O contract | `WorkflowSummaryResponse` + `inputSchema`, `outputSchema`, `protocols`, `triggerKinds` (from `declaredIoSchemas`, `core-contract.ts:439-456`, already computed for `describeWorkflow`) | `WorkflowSummary` mirrors them; `hope.workflows.schema(slug)` → `GET /workflows/{slug}/schema` (`workflow:definition:read`, already in the manifest) | `WORKFLOW_ENDPOINTS.SCHEMA`; `useWorkflowRun().schema(slug)` |
| Invoke a published agent from the browser (OD-F) | none (routes exist; §3.13 makes them metered) | none (`hope.agents.invoke/invokeAndStream/synthesize/transcribe`, `resources/agents.ts:129-210`, exists) | `AGENT_ENDPOINTS.INVOKE / SPEECH / TRANSCRIBE`; `useAgentInvocation()` — `invoke(slug, input, { mode })` (blocking JSON or SSE via `SSEClient`), refuses `RESERVED_RUN_IDENTITY_KEYS` client-side like `useWorkflowRun.ts:24,41-46`; works on an API key holding `agent:invocation:write` (+ `agent:definition:read` for listing) — the scopes exist and are mintable alone (§2.7 #10) |

| Sign an inbound webhook trigger | (exists: `signWebhookTrigger`, `workflow-exposure.service.ts:85-87`) | `signWebhookTrigger(secret, timestamp, rawBody)` + `WEBHOOK_TRIGGER_SIGNATURE_HEADER = 'X-Hope-Signature'`, `WEBHOOK_TRIGGER_TIMESTAMP_HEADER = 'X-Hope-Timestamp'` in `core/webhook-signature.ts` beside the existing `verifyWebhookSignature` (the SHA-256/HMAC core is reused; still zero runtime deps) | — |
| Socket lane | `modesFor` maps `socket` → `'socket'` (`workflow-schema-description.ts:43-48`) so the schema description stops lying; WS clients are TASK-898 | — | — |
| See the endpoints at publish | `describeWorkflow` already computes them | — | — (console, §3.10) |
| **No management surface (OD-F + OD-K)** | — | unchanged: `hope.admin.*` is service-account-only and generated | **REMOVED, not deprecated** (L9): the 33 endpoint-constant groups that embed `/admin/` (§2.7 #2 — `PROMPT_TEMPLATE_ENDPOINTS`, `TENANT_ENDPOINTS`, `USER_ENDPOINTS`, `ROLE_ENDPOINTS`, `TENANT_BUCKET_ENDPOINTS`, `PIPELINE_ENDPOINTS`, `DEPARTMENT_ENDPOINTS`, `GLOBAL_SETTINGS_ENDPOINTS`, `API_KEY_ENDPOINTS`, `QUEUE_ADMIN_ENDPOINTS`, `POLICY_ENDPOINTS`, `ENTITLEMENTS_ENDPOINTS`, `DNA_STYLE_ENDPOINTS`, `HARNESS_ADMIN_ENDPOINTS`, `AUDIT_LOG_ENDPOINTS`, `ADMIN_USER_*`, `TENANT_STORAGE_CONFIG_ENDPOINTS`, `RATE_LIMIT_ADMIN_ENDPOINTS`, `MONITORING_ENDPOINTS`, `STORAGE_KEY_ENDPOINTS`, `PLATFORM_METRICS_ENDPOINTS`, `ADMIN_TRANSCRIPTION_JOB_ENDPOINTS`, `ADMIN_CONSULTATION_ENDPOINTS`, `TENANT_FRONTEND_CONFIG_ENDPOINTS`, `PSTUDIO_ENDPOINTS`, `SERVICE_HEALTH_ENDPOINTS`, the single admin entries inside `WORKFLOW_ENDPOINTS` / `STT_ENDPOINTS` / `USER_SETTINGS_ENDPOINTS`), the 25 admin hook files and their tests (`useUserSettings` stays — self-only, `/users/me/settings`), their lines in `hooks/index.ts` / `core.ts`, and the README rows. `isAdminPlanePath` (`constants.ts:21`) is KEPT and inverted: `AgenticClient` REFUSES an `/admin/*` endpoint with a named error (the business-plane fence lives in the client too), so `AgenticClient.ts:1291`'s impersonation branch becomes a refusal. Consumers of the removed hooks: none first-party (§2.7 #2); external consumers get the 2.1 SDK changelog entry "management moved to `@arcaai/vox-node` `hope.admin.*` / the admin console" |
